# Decisions Archive

> **Append-only journal.** Closeout summaries for landed clusters + decisions rolled off `PROJECT-STATE.md`'s top-20 `Recent decisions` window. Reverse-chronological. Not read at session start; consulted when tracing *why* a prior decision was made.

Conventions:
- One H2 per event, dated `## YYYY-MM-DD — <one-line subject>`.
- Body is the original prose verbatim — do not rewrite.
- Newest entry on top.
- Struck-through backlog items roll up under a `## Backlog roll-up YYYY-MM-DD` header.

---

## 2026-08-20 — Cluster O Phase O1 closed: `ActionContextController` extracted, seven silent no-ops fixed

Implemented the plan's O1.T1-O1.T8 ("Correctness first: kill the silent no-ops") from `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-20-cluster-o-context-sensitive-ui.md`, following the audit report `docs/audit-2026-08-20-context-sensitive-ui.md`'s §3-4 bug citations. Three commits, ordered so each is independently buildable: `7c9faf91` lands O1.T3 (polymorphic zoom dispatch — `MarkdownView::zoomIn/Out/Reset` now scale the active leaf's font instead of being stale empty TODOs; `CanvasFileView` and `GraphView` override the same virtuals onto their real `CanvasView`/`ForceGraphView` viewport transforms via new `canvasView()`/`graphView()` accessors on their tab widgets; `ForceGraphView`'s wheel/keyboard zoom now route through the same `zoomIn()`/`zoomOut()` so there's one definition of the zoom factor) plus a new `tst_graphview_plugin::zoomDispatchesToViewportTransform`; `c3fbf00d` lands O1.T5/O1.T8's leaf-level primitives (`BasesView::focusSearch()`, `canUndo()`/`canRedo()`); `bbe1c658` lands the controller itself.

`ActionContextController` (new `src/app/ActionContextController.{h,cpp}`) absorbs five previously-scattered `MainWindow` refresh functions (`refreshEditorActions`, `updateEditorActionStates`, `updateVaultActions`, `updateBackForwardActions`, `updateTabStateActions`) plus the action half of the `activeLeafChanged` mega-lambda, closing the 2026-06-10 MainWindow-decomposition spec's deferred action-framework step. `MainWindow` keeps `actionCollection()` ownership (a `KXmlGuiWindow` requirement) and hands the controller a pointer. Per-task fixes: O1.T2 — the controller now also subscribes to `WorkspaceLeaf::viewChanged` (not just `Workspace::activeLeafChanged`), so an in-place view-type swap (e.g. clicking a `[[foo.canvas]]` wikilink, which navigates the same leaf rather than opening a new one) refreshes action state instead of leaving stale Format/Heading enablement and a stuck editor-mode radio behind — `Workspace::setActiveLeaf` early-returns on an unchanged leaf pointer, so nothing but back/forward previously listened to `viewChanged`. O1.T4 — `file_save` (Ctrl+S) now actually saves canvas tabs; canvas previously only ever saved on tab close, so Ctrl+S was a real no-op there. O1.T5 — `edit_find` routes to `BasesView::focusSearch()` on a bases tab (which already owns its own search box); `edit_replace`/`edit_find_next`/`edit_find_prev`/`insert_template` disable off-markdown rather than silently doing nothing. O1.T6 — merged `refreshEditorActions` and `updateEditorActionStates`, which used to disagree (`isMarkdown` vs `hasEditing()`) and relied on call order for the stricter one to win by accident; also fixes Insert ▸ Table being wrongly enabled in read-only Reading mode. O1.T7 — `view_source_mode` is now gated (it was omitted from `updateVaultActions` entirely, so it was always enabled regardless of context). O1.T8 — `edit_undo`/`edit_redo` enablement now reflects the active view's real undo-stack depth (bases `QUndoStack`, canvas `QUndoStack`) instead of being permanently enabled.

New `tests/app/tst_action_context.cpp` is the phase's acceptance gate: `noSilentNoop()` drives a real `MainWindow` + vault across four contexts (no vault open, markdown tab, canvas tab, bases tab) and asserts every currently-enabled action in `actionCollection()` has a real handler per `ActionContextController::hasHandlerForCurrentContext()` — an introspection table the controller maintains alongside its enablement logic, so drift between "enabled" and "has a handler" fails the build rather than shipping silently. Also covers O1.T2 (`inPlaceViewTypeChange_refreshesActionState`) and O1.T4 (`saveAction_savesCanvas`) by name, as the plan specifies. New `tests/app/tst_view_zoom_dispatch.cpp` covers markdown/canvas zoom (graph zoom's coverage lives in the graph plugin's own test binary, since `GraphView` is a separate plugin target this one doesn't link against).

317/317 offscreen in the final full-suite run. One incidental bug fixed along the way, found by the new graph-view zoom test itself rather than by production-path testing: `zoomDispatchesToViewportTransform()` originally declared `GraphView view(nullptr);` before `QWidget host;` and called `view.open(&host)`, which reparents `view` under `host` via `setParent()`. Locals destruct in reverse declaration order, so `host` (declared second) destructed *first*, and — now `view`'s Qt-ownership parent — deleted it; `view`'s own stack destructor then ran a second time on already-freed memory (SIGSEGV in `free()`). Fixed by declaring `host` before `view`, not by touching `GraphView`/`ForceGraphView`/`View::open()`, since the bug was entirely in the test's local lifetime, not the production dispatch code. One process-note for future O-phase sessions: a first `ctest -j10` run showed 2 spurious failures in `tst_action_context` (`view_zoom_in` reported enabled-with-no-handler in the `no-vault` and `bases` contexts; the editor-mode radio appeared to not clear after an in-place view swap) that did not reproduce across 3 standalone reruns nor the final full-suite pass — judged the same `-j10` parallel-load contention class as the already-documented `tst_canvas_perf_500` flake (both are `MainWindow`/vault-open-heavy integration tests), not a logic bug, but flagged here rather than silently written off.

**Next: Phase O2** (Tier-B capability contract — `View::canEdit()`/`canSave()`/`canZoom()`/`canFind()`/`hasSelection()`/`canUndo()`/`canRedo()` virtuals plus a `View::contextChanged()` signal, so O1's controller stops hand-checking each view subclass and instead asks the focused view what it can currently do).

## 2026-08-19 — Cluster M Phase M3 closed: edge authoring, live-verified (segfault found + fixed)

Five subagent-driven TDD tasks landed the full edge-authoring arc from the plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-19-cluster-m-canvas-authoring-parity.md`), one commit per task, `bfec72c8` bundling all five plus a docs-ticking commit `a050db75`, followed by a live-eyeball fix `d93f10e2`.

**M3.1 — Obsidian-exact bezier.** New `Canvas::CanvasBezierStrategy : Graffodil::EdgePathStrategy` replaces Graffodil's stock `BezierPathStrategy`: control-point offset `clamp(dist/2, 70, 150)` (vs. stock's `min(dist*0.4, 80)`) along each anchor's `outwardDirection`, plus a 7px face-inset stub at each end (drawn unconditionally — an arrowhead's ~10.4px height fully covers it when present, and it's what makes the line reach the face when there isn't one). New `Canvas::CanvasArrowTerminus` implements Obsidian's exact 13×10.4px triangle (stock `TriangleTerminus` was confirmed ~20-30% smaller and at a shallower angle via direct geometry comparison — half-angle 30° vs. Obsidian's ~32°, hypotenuse 10 vs. ~12.26).

**M3.2 — Drag-from-anchor edge creation.** Wired Graffodil's `CreateEdgeTool` (`setDragOnly(true)` for Obsidian-strict release-cancels-instead-of-click-click) into `CanvasScene`'s `CompositeTool` via the ready-made `addAnchorRoute(tool, 12.0)` primitive — Graffodil already shipped exactly the anchor-proximity routing this needed. `edgeRequested` → build a `CanvasEdge` (only id/nodes/sides set, defaults left absent per the disk-contract discipline) → `CmdAddEdge`. Works on all three node kinds by construction (`IGraphNode::nodeId()`, no `dynamic_cast`-per-type).

**M3.3 — Drop-on-empty create-and-connect.** `edgeDroppedOnEmpty` → popup menu ("New text card"/"New file card…"/"Cancel") → `CanvasScene::addCardConnectedTo()` (new public method, mirrors `createFileCardViaPicker`'s public-for-testability precedent): one compound undo step (`CmdAddCard` + `CmdAddEdge` parented under one `QUndoCommand`) computing the new card's facing side via the existing `pickSideToward()` helper (promoted out of `CanvasDocument.cpp`'s anonymous namespace to share it).

**M3.4 — Endpoint reconnect (the hard one).** Investigating "route reconnect-drag ahead of create-edge-drag" surfaced a real structural constraint in `Graffodil::CompositeTool`: `addAnchorRoute()` always `prepend()`s regardless of call order, and a plain `addMouseRoute()` always appends — so a reconnect route registered the ordinary way could *never* be tried before the anchor-routed create-edge tool, no matter what order the registration calls ran in. Worse, `addAnchorRoute`'s matcher (`isNearAnchor`) tests proximity to any *node* anchor, which is geometrically identical to "near an edge's terminus" (M3.1 draws termini exactly at the anchor position) — so even a second `addAnchorRoute` call couldn't discriminate the two gestures. Resolved without touching the Graffodil submodule: a new `Canvas::CanvasEdgeGestureTool` wraps both `CreateEdgeTool` and a new `Canvas::ReconnectEdgeTool`, registered as the single anchor-routed tool; on press it hit-tests all scene edges' termini (8px) and internally dispatches to reconnect or create. `ReconnectEdgeTool` mirrors `CreateEdgeTool`'s preview mechanics (dashed line + `AnchorHighlight`); on release it emits `reconnectRequested`/`reconnectDroppedOnEmpty`, never touching `CanvasDocument` directly. New `CmdReconnectEdge` (oldEdge/newEdge diff, `updateEdge()` on redo/undo). Also fixed a real gap in `CanvasScene::onEdgeChanged`: it only ever called `EdgeItem::setEdgeData()`, which cannot change *which* node an edge is attached to (`Graffodil::GraphEdgeItem` has no node-identity setter) — so a reconnect that changes `fromNode`/`toNode` would have updated the document correctly while leaving the visible edge silently pointing at the old node. Fixed by detecting a node-identity change and doing remove+recreate instead of in-place mutation.

**M3.5 — Direction menu.** New "Direction ▸ Nondirectional/Unidirectional/Bidirectional" submenu (new `CmdSetEdgeEnds`). Also closed a real bug while implementing this: the pre-existing "Reverse Direction" context-menu action was never on the undo stack at all — it mutated `CanvasDocument`/the scene directly with no `QUndoCommand` involved. Replaced with a new `CmdReverseEdge` (swap is self-inverse, so redo/undo share one code path) and wired the previously-dead `SelectMoveTool::reverseRequested` (R key) to the same logic — that signal had been connected to nothing since M1, per a code comment deferring it to "M3 (spec §3.6)".

**Live eyeball (same day): found + fixed a real segfault.** Right-click an edge → Edit Label → type → Enter crashed. Root cause: `QLineEdit::returnPressed` fires synchronously from inside the line edit's own `keyPressEvent` (`QWidgetLineControl::processKeyEvent`, still on the call stack), and the connected handler did `removeItem(proxy); delete proxy;` on the `QGraphicsProxyWidget` wrapping that same `QLineEdit` — a use-after-free once the handler returned into the now-freed call frame (backtrace: SIGSEGV in `QObjectPrivate::maybeSignalConnected`, reached via `editingFinished`'s activation inside the still-executing `processKeyEvent`). The group-label editor (`finishGroupLabelEdit`) had the byte-for-byte identical bug — same direct `returnPressed` connection, same synchronous delete — just not yet tripped live; fixed alongside it rather than waiting for it to be independently rediscovered. Both now call `removeItem()` synchronously (safe — it only detaches from the scene) and `proxy->deleteLater()` in place of the raw `delete`, which is this codebase's and Qt's standard idiom for destroying an object safely from within its own event handler. Commit `d93f10e2`. User confirmed the fix live and confirmed every other edge-authoring flow (draw, label, reconnect, redirect, delete) working.

**Verification:** 155/155 offscreen throughout all five tasks and the fix. Closed only after the live pass — consistent with this project's standing rule that canvas/editor interaction work is never closed on offscreen-green alone (project memory: an offscreen-green keyboard-focus fix was previously found broken live).

**Next:** Phase M4 (move/snap/selection polish — grid/object snapping via a new `CanvasAlignmentStrategy`, Shift axis/aspect-lock, arrow-key nudge at grid pitch, edge auto-pan while dragging near the viewport edge). Plan: [`superpowers/plans/2026-08-19-cluster-m-canvas-authoring-parity.md`](superpowers/plans/2026-08-19-cluster-m-canvas-authoring-parity.md).

## 2026-08-19 — Cluster M Phases M0-M2 closed: Graffodil rebase + node-creation flows, all live-verified

Full arc of Cluster M's substrate migration and first authoring milestone, executed same day via dispatched subagents with live human verification gating each phase close (project memory: canvas/editor interaction fixes are never closed on offscreen-green alone).

**M0 — Graffodil readiness gate.** Graffodil replied same day to the adoption handoff: submodule consumption blessed (no need to wait on 6d/CMake install-export), pin `v0.2.3`, and the M3 edge-gesture gap was fixed upstream ahead of need. `libs/graffodil` submodule added, wired via `add_subdirectory`, 313/313 offscreen.

**M1 — Substrate rebase, feature-frozen.** `libs/canvas` rebased onto `Graffodil::Core`: new `CanvasNodeItem`/`CanvasEdgeItem` base classes, `CanvasScene : Graffodil::GraphScene`, a bespoke `Graffodil::CompositeTool` assembly (Graffodil's own `DefaultGraphTool` couldn't be used as-is — its constructor pre-registers routes, leaving no room to prepend the resize-tool predicate), and full undo-wiring discipline (tools never touch `CanvasDocument` directly). Landed across two commits (`00d89904`, `6f476273`), 313/313 offscreen with 19 test slots (13 ported + 6 new). Live eyeball (M1.7) then found and fixed two bugs: app-wide Ctrl+Z/Redo never checked for an active canvas tab (`MainWindow::activeCanvasView()`, `fb672574`), and PNG/SVG export rendered black instead of matching the on-screen white background (`CanvasScene` never set `backgroundBrush()`, `2e06b966`). Both user-confirmed fixed; the headline file-card-unselectable bug (the reason M1 was undertaken) is structurally fixed by the rebase.

**M2 — Node creation flows.** All six creation UX flows landed across six commits (`10175197`..`92cab583`): double-click-empty → text card born in edit mode; context-menu "New file card…" via new `CanvasFilePickerDialog` (fuzzy-filtered, reusing the same `FuzzyMatcher` `CompletionPopup`/`QuickSwitcher` use); drag-drop from the OS/FileExplorer; clipboard copy/cut/paste with full re-ID round-trip through `.canvas`-shaped JSON (matching what Obsidian itself puts on the system clipboard); Alt-drag duplicate via new `CanvasDuplicateDragTool`; and a "Create new canvas" command built on a newly-generalized `FileManager::createNewFile`. User live-tested all six against the running dev build and confirmed every flow works, surfacing three more bugs fixed same session: a heap-corruption double-delete from destroying `CanvasScene` mid-edit (`8bfefddc` — see below), the file-picker's raw `QDirIterator` scan surfacing `.obsidian` internals as pickable files (`3a0fe634`), and Alt-drag committing a duplicate on a zero-movement click (`ddde36d5`).

**The heap-corruption bug, in detail:** `beginInlineEdit()`/`beginGroupLabelEdit()` connect `QApplication::focusChanged` back to `CanvasScene` so losing focus auto-commits an open edit. `QGraphicsScene::~QGraphicsScene()` deletes scene items — including a still-open edit proxy, if the scene is destroyed mid-edit (e.g. closing a tab without finishing the edit first) — before that connection is torn down (Qt's own auto-disconnect-on-destroy happens later, in `~QObject()`). Deleting the focused `QTextEdit`/`QLineEdit` synchronously re-emits `focusChanged`, re-entering `finishInlineEdit()`/`finishGroupLabelEdit()` mid-teardown and double-deleting an item the base-class destructor was already in the middle of deleting. Fixed with an explicit `CanvasScene` destructor that finishes any open edit *before* `QGraphicsScene`'s own item cleanup starts, while the scene is still fully intact.

**Remaining known gaps, punch-listed rather than fixed in-phase** (`docs/punch-list.md`, tag `[canvas][cluster-m]`): the file picker still isn't `Vault`-backed (no `Vault*` reachable at `CanvasViewTab`'s layer, only a `vaultRoot` string) — dot-path exclusion was added as a partial fix; canvas has no dark-mode/theme awareness anywhere (never consults `Corbomite::Core::ThemeService`) — surfaced when export background defaulted to black instead of matching the view's hardcoded white.

**Verification:** full offscreen suite green throughout (154 ctest binaries, 2 flaky-under-parallel-load timing tests confirmed passing standalone). Every phase closed only after a live human pass against the running dev build — no phase was closed on offscreen-green alone.

**Next:** Phase M3 (edge authoring — Obsidian-exact bezier, hover connection points + drag-to-create, drop-on-empty create-and-connect, endpoint reconnect, direction menu). Plan: [`superpowers/plans/2026-08-19-cluster-m-canvas-authoring-parity.md`](superpowers/plans/2026-08-19-cluster-m-canvas-authoring-parity.md).

User redirected from packaging work and signed off skipping Cluster K Phase 4 (soft "dogfood-as-default" with reversible toggle) in favour of Phase 5 (full QML retirement). LivePreview is now solely `Markoff::Canvas::EditorWidget`.

**Corbomite changes:**
- `NoteEditorWidget` always constructs canvas; removed `Markoff::Live::EditorWidget` / `editor()` accessor; added leaf-agnostic `linkService()`.
- Dropped `markoff_live` / `markoff_liveplugin` / `markoff_liveplugin_init` from `src/CMakeLists.txt`; removed `Quick`/`QuickWidgets`/`Qml` from top-level `find_package(Qt6)`.
- Removed `CanvasLivePreview` kcfg entry + Settings dialog toggle; `ReadableLineWidth` kept (now always applies).
- `MainWindow` insert-callout/table guards use `activeLeaf()` instead of `editor()`.
- Tests retargeted (`tst_link_activation`, hover, doc-lifetime, completion, title-rename); `tests/editor/CMakeLists.txt` links `Markoff::Canvas` instead of `markoff_live`.

**Markoff submodule:**
- New `MARKOFF_BUILD_LIVE` option (default ON for standalone Markoff; Corbomite forces OFF). Demo apps gated on LIVE.

Fixed by also calling `m_view->setDocument(nullptr)` + `m_actionController->setDocument(nullptr)`.

## 2026-08-18 — Cluster L Phase L5: Obsidian round-trip nested-split scrambling found and fixed

First real Phase L5 soak-dogfood finding. The user built a 3-pane test vault in real Obsidian (`~/Documents/WindowArrange...`): `TopLeft` in the top-left, `BottomLeft`/`BottomLeftTwo` stacked bottom-left below a horizontal splitter, and `RightFull` + a Graph View tab sharing the entire right column. Opening that vault in Corbomite scrambled every pane except `TopLeft` — first report had `BottomLeft`/`BottomLeftTwo` on the right and `RightFull`+Graph in the bottom-left; a fresh copy showed the same underlying corruption. Root causes, both in `libs/core/src/WorkspaceSerializer.cpp`:

1. **Direction convention inverted.** Obsidian's `direction: "vertical"|"horizontal"` on a split node names the orientation of the *divider line*, not the stacking axis of its children — `"vertical"` = left↔right siblings, `"horizontal"` = top↔bottom siblings (confirmed against `docs/obsidian-audit/domains/workspace.md` §WorkspaceParent/§SplitNode, and against a real Obsidian-authored `workspace.json`). `directionToKddwLocation` (the JSON→KDDW read path) and `walkLayoutContainer`'s Qt-orientation mapping (the KDDW→JSON write path) both had this backwards, with code comments asserting the wrong convention as fact — a self-consistent inversion, so a Corbomite-only save/reload round trip looked correct throughout; only cross-app interop with genuine Obsidian JSON exposed it.
2. **Anchor-threading order bug in `materializeSplit`.** The original code returned `firstAnchor` (the first-placed widget of a subtree) as the anchor for a caller docking that subtree's next sibling — wrong, because KDDW's `addDockWidget(location, relativeTo)` only ever splits the single frame `relativeTo` points at, not that frame's whole containing subtree. A first fix attempt (returning `anchorForNext`, the *last*-placed widget, instead) still produced a wrong layout when live-tested, because it also needed the *order* of operations fixed: a split's own later siblings must be placed at its own level **before** recursing into any nested child's internal structure — recursing first carves up a cell that the outer level hasn't isolated yet.

Fixed by restructuring materialization into a clean two-phase seed-then-fill algorithm: `firstLeafTabs()` is a pure (non-materializing) DFS that finds a split's leaf-most-first tabs node; `materializeSplit()` seeds that single leaf group at the caller's anchor; `fillSplitChildren()` then places the rest of *this* split's siblings (using the seed as anchor, matching KDDW's same-orientation-extends-the-existing-splitter behaviour) and only afterward recurses into each split-type child's own internals (including child 0) — by which point that child's cell is already correctly isolated from its outer siblings. Also fixed the two inverted-direction mapping sites and their misleading comments.

Took two live-test round trips against the real vault to land: the first attempt fixed only the direction inversion (still had the ordering bug) and the user caught a *different* wrong layout (`TopLeft` spanning the full top, `BottomLeft`/`BottomLeftTwo` correct bottom-left, `RightFull`+Graph in the bottom-right) — good diagnostic signal that narrowed straight to the anchor-ordering bug. The second attempt (siblings-then-recurse order) matched Obsidian's rendering exactly, user-confirmed live.

Added `fixture16_nestedSplitFirstChild_matchesObsidianGeometry` (new fixture `16-nested-split-first-child.json`, same shape as the real repro) to `tst_workspace_serializer.cpp` — it asserts actual on-screen widget adjacency (relative x/y positions via `KDDockWidgets::QtCommon::View_qt::asQWidget(...)->mapTo(...)`), not JSON shape. This matters: the pre-existing `fixture03_nestedSplits_threeDockWidgetsInCorrectGroups` test only checks dock-widget/group *counts* and that the re-serialized JSON has a plausible-looking split/tabs shape — it passed throughout this bug's entire lifetime and would never have caught it, since a scrambled layout can still round-trip a structurally normal-looking tree. (`height()` reports 0 for empty offscreen leaf widgets in this test harness, so the geometry assertions are x-position/y-order based rather than height-span based — still sufficient to distinguish both observed wrong layouts from the correct one, verified by hand-tracing both failure modes against the assertions.)

311/312 offscreen (excl. benchmark) — same single pre-existing `tst_e2e_gui::testSaveShortcut` flake, no regressions. Cluster L Phase L5 continues: remaining soak scenarios are drag-between-groups, split, popout, close-undo, vault switch, before closeout (retro + `PARITY-MATRIX.md` update per the plan file).

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster L Phase L4 code landed: native UX polish (live verification still pending)

Four commits: `9fa87398` (D1), `8fddb2b5` (D2), `0e6f4906` (D3), `03dd494e` (D4). Baseline going in and final state both 311/312 offscreen (excl. benchmark), sole failure the known `tst_e2e_gui::testSaveShortcut` flake — no regressions. **This entry records code landed, not a closed phase** — D1 and D4 explicitly need a live dogfood pass (project memory: offscreen-green history has previously hidden a broken keyboard-focus fix; do not repeat that here).

**D1 (KDDW chrome) — landed, needs live eyeball.** Added `Flag_HideTitleBarWhenTabsVisible` + `Flag_ShowButtonsOnTabBarIfTitleBarHidden` to `KDDockWidgets::Config` (eliminates the redundant title-bar row `AlwaysShowTabs` alone was producing, keeps close/float/maximize reachable on the tab bar) and middle-click-to-close via an app-wide `Workspace::eventFilter` scoped to `isAncestorOf(m_kddwMain)` (KDDW has no built-in flag for this; tab bars are created/destroyed dynamically per group so app-wide + scoping was the only stable hook). Tab-bar KDE-palette styling and drop-indicator legibility: no code bug found (no hardcoded colors overriding the palette), so nothing was "fixed" there — those are purely visual and genuinely unassessable offscreen.

**D2 (back/forward completion) — landed.** `Ctrl+Alt+Left/Right` via `KActionCollection`, mouse buttons 4/5 (`Qt::BackButton`/`Qt::ForwardButton`) in the same event filter as D1, and `MainWindow::updateBackForwardActions()` binding the global actions to the active leaf's `LeafHistory::canGoBack()`/`canGoForward()`, re-subscribed on every `activeLeafChanged` so state can't go stale mid-leaf. Lower live-risk than D1 (action-enablement wiring, not chrome/focus), but keyboard/mouse-button interaction itself hasn't been manually driven either.

**D3 (missing tab commands) — landed.** `Ctrl+1..8`/`Ctrl+9` jump-to-tab on existing `tabGroupIdOf`/`groupMembers`; pin-tab as a one-call wrapper over pre-existing `WorkspaceLeaf::pinned/setPinned` + `propagatePinToGroup` (no default shortcut, matching Obsidian/Kate's menu-only precedent); move-to-new-window wrapping `Workspace::popoutLeaf`; toggle-stacked decided **advisory-only, not hide** — KDDW has no stacked-tabs rendering mode to hook, so the action flips the existing round-tripped `stacked` bit and posts a status-bar message rather than being silently dead.

**D4 (sidebar behavior) — landed, needs live eyeball.** Root cause: Phase L2 built the tier-2/3 width *storage* but nothing wired real values through — `leftVisible` restore existed, widths didn't, and the save call passed literal `200, false, 200`. Fixed via `CorbomiteMDI::Sidebar::lastSize()`/`setLastSize()` + `MainWindow::sidebarWidth()`/`setSidebarWidth()`, with real widths now saved and restored on vault-open. Deliberately did **not** resurrect `CorbomiteMDI`'s separate dormant `startRestore`/`finishRestore`/`saveSession` KConfig mechanism — it persists an overlapping-but-different shape that would compete with the L2 tier model. Keyboard toolview focus turned out to already exist (`GUIClient::registerToolView`'s per-toolview "Focus %1" action, same unbound-by-design pattern as upstream Kate) — no change needed. KXMLGUI "Index 18" merge-index noise left on the punch list (`[ui][xmlgui][P5]`, root cause already identified there) rather than an unscoped dig.

**Test bar:** 311/312 offscreen, same single pre-existing flake, no new failures. **Not independently driven with mouse/keyboard by the agent** for any of D1-D4 — all four are build+offscreen-test verified only.

**Next:** live dogfood pass on D1 (title-bar removal, tab styling, drop indicators) and D4 (width restore on a real vault open) before this phase can close. D2/D3 are lower-risk but also unverified live. After that: Phase L5 (soak & closeout dogfood session, files `[cluster-l]` punch-list findings, updates `PARITY-MATRIX.md` workspace rows).

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster L Phase L3 landed: cruft removal (C6 deliberately skipped)

Four commits: `0060cedc` (C1), `0b3bd503` (C3), `98204f9b` (C4), `2f4cd239` (C5 partial). Baseline going in: 311/312 offscreen (excl. benchmark), sole failure the known `tst_e2e_gui::testSaveShortcut` flake. No regressions from any change below.

**C1 (dead Obsidian-shape shells) — fixed.** Deleted `WorkspaceRoot`/`WorkspaceContainer`/`WorkspaceSidedock` (headers, sources, CMakeLists entries) after `grep -rn` confirmed no real callers outside their own dedicated test — `WorkspaceContainer` never stored children, `WorkspaceRoot` was constructed but never populated, `leftSplit()`/`rightSplit()` always returned literal `nullptr` (the exact "compiling ≠ compatible" crash-invitation the L0 doctrine calls out). `WorkspaceFloating`/`WorkspaceWindow` were **kept** — confirmed live callers backing the real popout-window lifecycle (`popoutLeaf`, `reparentToMain`, `floatingSplit()->windows()`). No overlap with `2026-06-10-release-hygiene.md`'s dead-code purge.

**C3 (duplicated `ensureKddwInit`) — fixed.** Two identical static-guard copies (`Workspace.cpp`, `WorkspaceLeaf.cpp`) consolidated into `Corbomite::detail::ensureKddwInit()` in new `libs/core/src/WorkspaceKddwInit.h/.cpp`.

**C4 (router O(chain × leaves) perf) — fixed.** `WorkspaceActiveLeafRouter::onFocusChanged` now early-outs via `mainWin->isAncestorOf(current)` before any leaf walk, and fetches `allLeaves()` once instead of re-copying it per ancestor. New test `focusOutsideMainWindowDoesNotChangeActiveLeaf`.

**C5 (undebounced event mirrors) — partial, by design.** `Workspace::resize()` now coalesces `QEvent::Resize` bursts via a zero-delay `QTimer::singleShot` guard (Obsidian-parity debounce); new test `rapidResizeBurstCoalescesToOneEmission`. **`activeLeafChanged` deliberately left synchronous**: it has real synchronous production consumers (MainWindow title/ribbon, `BasesView` refresh, link-resolver freshness) and existing tests assert exact-count emission without pumping the event loop — debouncing it is a workspace-wide UX-timing change, judged too broad to live-verify in one session for a signal no plugin currently hooks.

**C6 (`m_tabGroupOf`/`m_stackedGroups` cache slimming) — skipped, per the plan's own escape hatch.** More entangled than the plan's framing suggested: `m_tabGroupOf` has two live production uses beyond the serializer key — `WorkspaceSerializer::walkLayoutContainer`'s `stacked`-bit lookup, and `Workspace::deserialize`'s correlation of live KDDW group membership back to the JSON's original grouping for deferred-tab materialization, a path Phase L1 specifically hardened after a real dogfood bug. Deriving both from live `DockRegistry::groups()` is possible but touches that crash-sensitive path for a marginal cleanliness win — left alone rather than forced through, honoring the plan's explicit "err toward NOT touching it if the win is marginal and the blast radius is workspace-wide."

**`eState.scroll` (B6) — confirmed accurate on the punch list, not fixed.** Traced to `Markoff::MarkdownView::scrollPositionVisualLine()`, a 0.0–1.0 fraction that's part of the Markoff submodule's contract-v2 API (identical across all four leaves). Re-anchoring to a real visual-line number needs either a Markoff-side contract change or a Corbomite-side fraction↔line conversion requiring total-line-count context at both save and restore — genuinely bigger than this phase. Existing `[workspace][P4][phase1-2026-06-10]` punch-list entry already describes this correctly; left as-is.

**Test bar:** 311/312 offscreen, same single pre-existing flake, no new failures.

**Next:** Phase L4 (native UX polish — D1 KDDW chrome pass with live eyeball, D2 back/forward completion, D3 tab commands, D4 sidebar persistence revival) or Phase L5 (soak & closeout dogfood session). No hard ordering between them.

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster L Phase L2 landed: one writer, full fidelity (implements the L0 tier doctrine)

Implements the three-tier storage model from the L0 spec (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-17-workspace-compat-boundary.md`) in code. Three commits: `2f5d5760` (B3 id-assignment + B4 sidecar-bound), `13396015` (B1 full-fidelity writer + C2 promotion + `_corbomite`/`left-ribbon` denylist + golden fixture), `03511566` (`SessionManager` split into tier-2/tier-3 writers + `MainWindow` wiring).

**B1 (production dropped `floating`/`lastOpenFiles`) — fixed.** `Workspace::writeWorkspaceJson`/`readWorkspaceJson` promoted to the actual production path (closing C2 per the plan's stated preference), replacing `MainWindow`'s old main+active-only extraction. Full `serialize()` payload now flows through, including an `m_unknownRoot` passthrough for any root key Workspace doesn't model (future-Obsidian-key forward compat, per the L0 doctrine). `_corbomite` and `left-ribbon` are explicitly denylisted on load rather than swept into that passthrough — no migration needed (confirmed zero installed users), just stop writing them and drop them if found.

**Tier 2/3 split — built.** `SessionManager` (`src/app/SessionManager.h/.cpp`) rewritten to own two writers: tier 2 `.obsidian/corbomite/state.json` (expandedFolders, leftRibbon, sidebar visibility+activePanel, a `vaultId` UUID minted once on first open) and tier 3 `<AppDataLocation>/vaults/<vaultId>/session.json` (window geometry/state, sidebar pixel widths, ephemeral `pluginSessionState`). Dev/release isolation falls out for free from the existing `corbomite`/`corbomite-dev` `AppDataLocation` split (`KAboutData`).

**B4 (unbounded process-global sidecar statics) — fixed, simpler than planned.** Plan proposed converting `leafSidecar()`/`stackedSidecar()` to instance state on a serializer context object; landed fix instead clears both maps at the top of every `WorkspaceSerializer::fromJson()` parse — resolves the actual complaint (unbounded growth across vault switches) without the larger refactor. Documented deviation, judged sufficient.

**B3 (empty split/tabs ids, missing `dimension`) — partially fixed, gap punch-listed.** Id assignment (16-hex on write) is fully fixed and tested. `dimension` parsing was added, but does **not** survive a live load→save round trip: the production writer always rebuilds the split/tabs tree fresh from KDDW's `LayoutSaver` dump, which has no concept of the original ids/dimensions from the file that was loaded. Fixing this for real needs a durable id/dimension store correlated to KDDW's ephemeral split containers — explicitly permitted as a shortcut by the plan ("if lossy, at least round-trip the values unchanged"); filed to the punch list as `[cluster-l]` rather than silently closed.

**Golden test:** `tests/core/fixtures/workspace-obsidian/15-golden-full-fidelity.json` + coverage in `tst_workspace_session.cpp` for denylist-on-load, unknown-root passthrough, id assignment, and full load→save fidelity — the executable form of the L0 doctrine. `tests/storage/tst_session_manager_roundtrip.cpp` and `tests/dialogs/tst_ribbonstatecontroller.cpp` rewritten (old versions asserted the retired `_corbomite`/`left-ribbon`-in-`workspace.json` shape).

**Test bar:** 310/313 offscreen (excl. benchmark) — 2 failures, both confirmed unrelated and non-regressed: `tst_e2e_gui::testSaveShortcut` (pre-existing, already punch-listed) and a one-off `tst_forcelayout::testCoarseningQuality` timing/randomness flake (forcegraph library, nothing to do with workspace code; passed clean on isolated rerun).

**Next:** Phase L3 (cruft removal — C1 dead shells, C3 duplicate init, C4 router hash, C5 debounces, C6 tab-group cache, `eState.scroll` B6) or Phase L4 (native UX polish — D1-D4). Cluster K Phase 4 (dogfood-as-default) remains unblocked since L1.

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster L Phase 0+1 landed: compat-boundary doctrine signed off; teardown-UAF crash class fixed

**Phase L0 (doctrine, spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-17-workspace-compat-boundary.md`, Accepted):** established that Obsidian compatibility lives at the file-format boundary (`.obsidian/workspace.json` must round-trip Obsidian's exact schema, including unknown/future keys) and nowhere else — in-memory object shape, teardown order, and event timing are free to be idiomatic Qt/KDE. Concretized the B2 finding (Corbomite-private keys `_corbomite`/`left-ribbon` currently written into `.obsidian/workspace.json`, which Obsidian destroys wholesale on every session) into a **three-tier storage model**, refined during design review after confirming Corbomite has zero installed users (no migration shim needed — Phase L2 just stops writing the old keys and denylist-strips them on load): Tier 1 = genuine Obsidian-schema keys, stays in `workspace.json`. Tier 2 = vault-portable Corbomite-native preferences a user would reasonably expect to follow the vault between machines (expanded folders, ribbon layout, sidebar visibility) — new file `.obsidian/corbomite/state.json`, inside the vault but never touched by Obsidian, rides whatever sync channel (Syncthing/git) the vault already uses. Tier 3 = genuinely machine-local state (window geometry, sidebar pixel widths, ephemeral per-toolview `pluginSessionState`) — `~/.local/share/corbomite[-dev]/vaults/<vaultId>/session.json`, never synced. Found in review that `PluginDataStore` (`.obsidian/plugins/<id>/data.json`) already gives plugins a correct vault-portable home matching real Obsidian behavior, distinct from the narrower ephemeral `pluginSessionState` — motivated the tier-2/tier-3 split rather than lumping all "Corbomite-native" state into one machine-local blob. `vaultId` to be a UUID minted on first open and stored in tier 2 (stable across rename/move), not a path hash.

**Phase L1 (teardown unification, the crash-safety phase — landed before K Phase 4 per the plan's ordering requirement):** fixed finding A1, a use-after-free hit on every vault-open-with-saved-session: `Workspace::deserialize`/`resetToDefaultLayout` did synchronous `qDeleteAll(m_leaves)` with no `releaseDockWidget()` while KDDW's registry/layout still referenced the widgets, so dock-widget destruction could re-entrantly fire `isCurrentTabChanged`/`isOpenChanged` into half-cleared Workspace state mid-batch — same defect class as the 2026-06-10 first-run SIGSEGV. Introduced `Workspace::destroyLeaves(QVector<WorkspaceLeaf*>, TeardownMode::{Immediate,Deferred})` as the single teardown primitive (unregister-before-touch so "alive" == "registered" at every point in a batch, disconnect dock-widget signals, then delete/deleteLater per mode); routed `~Workspace`, `closeLeaf`, `deserialize`, and `resetToDefaultLayout` through it (`90d994f3`). Fixed A4 (`resetToDefaultLayout` wasn't clearing `m_stackedGroups`) in the same commit. Fixed A3 separately (`93eb8e01`): `wireLeafKddwSignals`'s re-emission lambdas now use `leaf` as the `QObject::connect` context object instead of relying solely on the `m_leavesById.contains()` convention-guard that A1 had been silently depending on — Qt auto-disconnects structurally on leaf destruction now. New tests: `deserializeManyLayoutsBackToBack_noCrashCorrectCounts` (8 rounds, flat/nested-split/floating/empty fixtures), `openCloseReopenCycle_noCrashAndConsistentState`, `resetToDefaultLayout_clearsStackedGroups` (confirmed via `git stash` to fail pre-fix, pass post-fix). Offscreen suite: 310/312 (excl. benchmark), 2 failures pre-existing/unrelated (`tst_e2e_gui::testSaveShortcut`, already punch-listed; `tst_canvas_perf_500`, a timing-budget flake under `-j10` load, not workspace code). **ASAN one-off build (`build-asan/`, ran all 11 `tst_workspace_*` binaries individually under `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen`): zero AddressSanitizer/LeakSanitizer diagnostics, all green** — the crash-safety exit gate from the plan is met.

**Next:** Phase L2 (one writer, full fidelity — implements the L0 doctrine: split `SessionManager` into the three tiers, denylist-strip `_corbomite`/`left-ribbon` on load, fix B3 empty ids/missing `dimension`, kill B4's unbounded sidecar statics, golden Obsidian-fixture round-trip test). Cluster K Phase 4 (dogfood-as-default) is now unblocked by L1 per the plan's ordering constraint, though L2/L3/L4 remain open.

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster K Phase 3 closed: live re-test confirms 10/14, surfaces + fixes a tab-navigation bug

Live re-test of the 5 findings fixed in "session 2" (below), against the running app with `CanvasLivePreview` on.

**4 confirmed working as-is:** F3 find-next scroll ("works great"), double/triple-click select ("works great"), Ctrl+Scroll zoom (confirmed), image/embed placeholders ("properly show placeholders").

**1 (wikilink click/middle-click) needed a second, deeper fix — outside the submodule.** The canvas-side click-activation fix (`7b386915`) was correct: plain click now activates a link when the caret is elsewhere. But the user's actual complaint was different from what got filed originally — every click, plain or middle, opened the target in a **new tab**, making plain click indistinguishable from middle-click, with the tab-frame's visible back/forward nav buttons never doing anything. Root cause, found by reading `MainWindow::openFileInWorkspace`: it only ever creates a new leaf or switches to one where the file is already open — there was no code path anywhere that navigated the *currently active* leaf in place. `WorkspaceLeaf::navigate()` (pushes to `LeafHistory`, which the nav buttons already read from via `ItemView.cpp`'s `goBack`/`goForward` connections) existed and worked, but nothing in the link-activation path had ever called it. This predates Cluster K entirely — Live and Styled leaves would have hit the identical bug, since `NoteEditorWidget::linkActivated` is the same leaf-agnostic signal for all three.

Fixed in two parts:
1. `Markoff::LinkActivation::openInNewTab` (added during the original click-activation fix, canvas commit `7b386915`) was being silently dropped in `NoteEditorWidget::onLinkActivated`'s conversion to the plain `linkActivated(QString)` signal — the signal signature had no way to carry it. Now `linkActivated(QString, bool openInNewTab)`.
2. `MainWindow` branches on the flag: `true` (middle-click) keeps existing `onNoteActivated`/`openFileInWorkspace` behavior; `false` (plain click) calls a new `navigateActiveLeafTo()`, which resolves/creates the target (shared `resolveOrCreateNoteTarget()` helper, factored out of `onNoteActivated` so the two paths can't drift) and calls the active leaf's `navigate()` directly.

Commit `44d9eb66` (Corbomite `src/`, not the submodule). Four call sites updated for the new signal arity: `MainWindow.cpp`'s connection lambda, and three test files (`tst_mainwindow_link_navigation.cpp` — plus two new regression tests asserting leaf count doesn't grow on a plain click but does on middle-click, and that `history().canGoBack()` becomes true after an in-place navigation; `tst_e2e_gui.cpp`).

**Also fixed this session, found by the same live pass:** Ctrl+=/Ctrl+-/Ctrl+0 keyboard zoom did nothing (only Ctrl+Scroll had been wired). Root cause: the QML Live leaf's keyboard zoom shortcuts are window-level QML `Shortcut` items, not KActions (MainWindow's own zoom-action wiring has a comment explaining why — binding these keys as KActions collides/is ambiguous with the QML shortcuts), so there was never a KAction path for canvas to inherit from, and canvas had no local-shortcut equivalent of its own. Fixed by adding the same three keys to `View::keyPressEvent`, reusing the `fontScaleStepRequested` signal `wheelEvent`'s Ctrl+Scroll fix (session 2, below) already established, plus a new `fontScaleResetRequested` for Ctrl+0. Deliberately kept as its own signal-based mechanism, not a KAction, for the same collision reason. Commit `03f7ceb1` (submodule).

Submodule re-pinned `1e45ae8e` → `03f7ceb1` (one commit: the Ctrl-key zoom fix; the tab-navigation fix is Corbomite-side only). markoff-family suite 316/316 (Ctrl-key fix verified against `-R canvas`, 39/39). Corbomite suite 154/154 offscreen (one `tst_canvas_perf_500` failure under `-j10` parallel contention, confirmed flaky/unrelated — passes standalone).

**10 of 14 Cluster K dogfood findings are now closed and live-confirmed.** Remaining 4 (callouts frozen on Markoff E3, document-title header, readable-line-width setting, format-verb reveal-radius) are all low-severity polish/feature-gap, not correctness bugs — see punch-list `[cluster-k]` for detail. Wrote a G2-closing status report to Markoff: [`docs/handoff/2026-08-17-to-markoff-canvas-g2-adoption-report.md`](handoff/2026-08-17-to-markoff-canvas-g2-adoption-report.md) — recommendation is to proceed to Phase 4 (dogfood-as-default) rather than pause for further upstream engine work; nothing found in two dogfood sessions suggested canvas itself needs more development before Corbomite continues.

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster K Phase 2/3: canvas dogfood pass, 5/14 findings fixed

First hands-on session with `Markoff::Canvas::EditorWidget` as an alternate LivePreview engine, behind the `CanvasLivePreview` settings toggle (Phase 1 wiring — `NoteEditorWidget`/`.h`, `corbomite.kcfg`, `SettingsDialog`, `src/CMakeLists.txt` — had already landed in the working tree from a prior session; this session built, tested, and dogfooded it). Launched Corbomite with the toggle on, exercised it against the starter vault, captured stdout/stderr to a log file for post-session analysis.

**The log came back empty.** Zero qWarning/qDebug/crash output for the entire session, including through two bugs that outright hung editing state (`$$` lockup, undo phantom-link) — confirming these were silent logic bugs, not caught-and-logged failures, and that nothing upstream had an assertion guarding the affected code paths.

**14 findings filed to punch-list `[cluster-k]`, severity-ranked P0–P6** (see punch-list.md for the full table). Per user direction, fixes were patched directly in the `libs/markoff-family` submodule rather than steered upstream via a handoff doc — joint local ownership of both repos makes that faster for bugs this size, and the fixes were verified with the submodule's own 306-test offscreen suite plus Corbomite's 152-test suite before each re-pin.

**Fixed and user-confirmed (5 of 14):**
1. **P0 — New Note unusable in canvas mode.** An empty-markdown load produced zero D2 blocks; every view assumes a document has somewhere for a caret to live, so canvas's `View::keyPressEvent` bailed on every keystroke. Fixed by synthesizing one empty Paragraph block on an empty load (`materializeBlocksFromParsedDoc`), mirroring `applyFlatEdit`'s existing empty-document handling on the interactive-edit ingress. (`9389fb21`)
2. **P1 — `$$` math-block lockup.** `StructuralKeyHandler` had no case at all for `BlockKind::Math`, so Enter/Backspace/Delete were silently unhandled — Enter did nothing, and Backspace/Delete at a boundary (reachable almost immediately in a 2-character line) hit a no-op that deferred to the very structural-key handling that had just declined it. First fix added a Math case but made Enter always insert a literal newline, which the user caught on re-test as a new regression: the block became a one-way trap, unable to ever produce a following paragraph. Second fix made Enter split into a new Paragraph at the end of the formula (same shape as `paragraphEnter`), keeping the literal-newline behavior only for an interior Enter, since display math can legitimately span lines (e.g. an `aligned` environment). (`9389fb21`, `794e28ec`)
3. **P1 — undo leaves a phantom "unresolved link" on unrelated text.** The `UndoLog` dispatcher's Buffer-target branch mutates the CRDT `Buffer` directly, bypassing `applyBlockEdit`/`d2ApplyBufferEdit` — the only other places that bump `blockEditSequences`. `InlineParseCache` is keyed on `(BlockId, blockEditSequence)`, so after an undo it kept serving link spans parsed from the pre-undo text until an unrelated edit finally forced a re-parse. Fixed by bumping the sequence in the dispatcher itself, on both undo and redo. (`9389fb21`)
4. **P2 — doubled `-` on new list items.** `promoteCaretBlockKind()` had promotion branches for Heading and Math but none for ListItem: the kind flipped, but the typed marker text was never stripped from the buffer and no MarkerStyle/MarkerNumber/IndentLevel attrs were set, so the reconstructed display marker doubled up with the still-present typed one. Fixed by porting the same marker-parse-then-strip-and-set-attrs logic `LiveListModelBinding` already uses for this promotion in the live leaf. (`a0dd2a08`)
5. **P2 — Tab leaks keyboard focus to the toolbar.** First fix added a literal-tab-insertion fallback inside `View::keyPressEvent`, verified by the offscreen suite — but the user's re-test immediately caught that it changed nothing in the running app. Root cause: `QWidget::event()` intercepts a plain Tab/Backtab `QKeyEvent` and calls `focusNextPrevChild()` *before* `keyPressEvent()` ever runs, whenever another widget can take focus — so the keyPressEvent-level fix was structurally unreachable, a gap the offscreen test suite couldn't see because it doesn't have a toolbar to focus into. Fixed by overriding `View::focusNextPrevChild()` to always return `false` (the same technique `QPlainTextEdit::setTabChangesFocus(false)` uses internally), so control always reaches `keyPressEvent()`. (`a0dd2a08`, `794e28ec`)

**9 findings remain open**, unchanged from filing: wikilink click/middle-click navigation semantics, F3 not scrolling to the next match, double/triple-click select unimplemented, Ctrl+Scroll not zooming, inconsistent bare-URL image embed rendering, callouts (pre-existing gap, frozen on Markoff E3), the document-title header not surfaced, no readable-line-width setting, and a wider-than-expected markup-reveal radius. All still severity-ranked at the top of the punch list under `[cluster-k]`.

**Process note for next session:** two of the five "fixed" items needed a second pass because the first pass's own regression tests passed while the actual behavior was still wrong or newly broken — in both cases an offscreen unit test verified the mechanism in isolation (Math's Enter-at-end split; Tab's literal-insertion code path) without exercising the surrounding context that made it fail in the real app (a following block to split into and check the kind of; a toolbar to steal focus to). Live user re-testing against the running app caught both. Neither fix ships from this branch as "verified" beyond what the app confirmed — treat the running-app confirmation, not the test suite, as the actual gate here.

Submodule advanced `94f661c3` → `794e28ec` (3 commits: `9389fb21`, `a0dd2a08`, `794e28ec`, all on a local `cluster-k-canvas-fixes` branch in the `libs/markoff-family` checkout — not pushed to Markoff's remote, since this was direct-patch-in-place work rather than an upstream contribution). markoff-family suite 306/306, Corbomite suite 152/152 offscreen (excl. realistic/benchmark/perf; one `tst_forcelayout` failure during a parallel run reproduced as a standalone pass, confirmed pre-existing/flaky and unrelated).

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster K Phase 3: 5 more canvas findings fixed (10/14 total)

Continuation of the same day's Phase 2/3 dogfood session above. Worked through the punch list's remaining `[cluster-k]` findings top-down, dispatching one general-purpose subagent per fix (or per tightly-related bundle) with TDD instructions, stacking each commit on the same local `cluster-k-canvas-fixes` branch in `libs/markoff-family` so the submodule only needed one re-pin at the end. Builds/tests were serialized (one agent at a time) since all agents shared the same submodule build directory.

**Fixed (5 of 9 remaining):**
1. **P2 — wikilink click/middle-click navigation semantics.** `mousePressEvent`'s link-activation gesture only checked `m_readOnly || Ctrl`, never the caret's own position, so a plain click while editing always just placed the caret regardless of where the caret currently sat — differing from Obsidian's actual rule (plain click navigates when the caret is elsewhere; only placing the caret inside the link's own revealed-raw-text line). Fixed by adding caret-block awareness mirroring the mechanism `isDelimiterHiddenAt` already uses for bold/italic delimiter reveal. Middle-click: added a link-activation branch ahead of the X11 primary-selection-paste guard, plus a new `LinkActivation::openInNewTab` field in `libs/markoff-core` (modifiers alone can't encode which mouse button fired). (`7b386915`)
2. **P3 — double-click word-select / triple-click paragraph-select unimplemented.** `View` had no `mouseDoubleClickEvent` at all. Added one using `QTextBoundaryFinder::Word` (same idiom as the existing `moveCaretByWord`) for double-click; since Qt has no native triple-click event, `mouseDoubleClickEvent` arms a same-block/within-`doubleClickInterval()` detector that `mousePressEvent` checks on the next press. (`b2bd4d60`)
3. **P3 — Ctrl+Scroll accelerated scrolling instead of zooming.** `View` had no `wheelEvent` override. Added one: Ctrl+Scroll now emits `fontScaleStepRequested(int steps)` (View has no font-scale authority of its own — that's `EditorWidget`'s, per contract-v2), applied via the same `fontScale() * 1.10` step already used by the View-menu Zoom In/Out actions. (`4f94e61b`)
4. **P2 — F3 find-next doesn't scroll the viewport to the next match.** Root cause: `ensureCaretVisible()`'s scrollbar math trusted `BlockLayoutCache`'s *estimated* block heights for blocks never through a real layout pass; a long wrapped paragraph's estimate badly undercounts, so once `paintEvent()` later replaced it with the real height (shifting every subsequent block's y), nothing re-checked whether the caret's target block was still actually in the viewport — repeated F3 looked frozen once the match scrolled off-screen. Fixed by splitting the scrollbar-clamp math into `scrollCaretIntoView()` and re-running it from `ensureLayoutForViewport()`'s realize loop (gated on a new `m_caretVisibilityPending` flag, cleared by `setScrollAnchor()` so an explicit ephemeral-state scroll restore still always wins). Regression test drives three `findNext()` calls across matches under 25/50 wrapped filler paragraphs; verified it fails without the fix. (`8e091a8f`)
5. **P3 — some remote image embeds render as an empty/invisible line until clicked into.** Neither of the two hypotheses filed with this finding (empty-alt-text special case; parse-level bug) was the actual cause — investigation found `BlockKind` promotion from `Paragraph` → `Image`/`Embed` only ever runs from `onDocumentChanged()` (an actual edit with the caret in that block); a bare document load never promotes any block but block 0, so *every* image/embed line loaded from a vault file and never typed into stays `BlockKind::Paragraph` forever, and an un-promoted Paragraph's `![...]` span gets fully hidden by `TreeSitterParser`'s link-delimiter-hiding rule (which never special-cased `image_description` as visible content, unlike `link_text`) — confirmed via instrumented runs that `![alt](url)`, `![](url)`, and `![[wikitarget]]` were *all* blank pre-promotion, not just the bare-URL case originally suspected. Fixed presentation-only (no document mutation): `BlockPresentation::presentationFor`'s `Paragraph` case now content-sniffs image/embed shape the same way it already does for footnote-def paragraphs. (`1e45ae8e`)

**4 findings remain open**: callouts render as empty lines (pre-existing gap, frozen on Markoff E3 — not actionable from this side), Markoff's document-title header not surfaced in Corbomite, no readable-line-width setting, and a wider-than-expected markup-reveal radius on caret-adjacency (all P4–P6, cosmetic/feature-gap rather than correctness bugs).

**Not yet user-confirmed.** Unlike the first 5 fixes (Phase 2, confirmed live against the running app), these 5 were verified only against the offscreen test suite by subagents working without access to a running GUI session — per this project's standing rule (see memory `feedback_verify_ui_fixes_live`), canvas/editor keyboard-and-mouse-interaction fixes need a live re-test before being called truly done, not just green tests. Flagged in the punch list as `[FIXED, pending live eyeball]` rather than `[RESOLVED]`.

Submodule advanced `794e28ec` → `1e45ae8e` (4 new commits on top of the existing `cluster-k-canvas-fixes` branch, fast-forward-merged to the submodule's local `master`; still not pushed to Markoff's remote). markoff-family suite 316/316, Corbomite suite 154/154 offscreen (excl. realistic/benchmark/perf).

## 2026-08-17 — Cluster K Phase 3: findings 11-13 closed (title-band rename, readable-line-width, reveal-radius); `feature/find-replace` merged to master

Knocked out the 3 remaining actionable `[cluster-k]` findings (the 4th, callouts, stays FROZEN — blocked on Markoff E3, out of scope for this cluster):

1. **Document-title header not surfaced.** Canvas already shipped the title band (`View::setInlineTitle`/`titleEdited`) — the gap was purely that nobody called it. `NoteEditorWidget` now seeds it from the note's filename on every attach/detach/mode-switch and on `NoteDocument::pathChanged` (external-rename resync); a debounced `titleEdited` (fires per keystroke) commits a real rename via a new `MainWindow::titleRenameRequested` handler → `FileManager::renameFileByPath`, rejecting a literal `/` in the typed text rather than silently turning it into a folder move. New test `tst_mainwindow_title_rename.cpp` (4 cases). Commit `0f3a8792`.
2. **No readable-line-width setting.** Turned out to be a pure wrapper-exposure gap — `View` already fully implemented `ContentWidthPolicy` (`FullWidth`/`FixedColumn(700px)`, Obsidian's own `--file-line-width` default). Added the `EditorWidget` pass-through (submodule `1fd3ef11`), a new `ReadableLineWidth` kcfg entry (canvas-only, default true), and an Editor-page checkbox that force-enables + disables "Wrap long lines" while checked, wired live through the existing `onSettingsApplied` chokepoint.
3. **Format-verb reveal radius too wide.** `InlineFormatting.cpp`'s `touchedByAnyCursor` padded the "caret touches this span" test by an extra `±1` QChar position on both sides. Fixed with an asymmetric interval (`parentCharStart < c <= parentCharEnd`): the gap immediately BEFORE a span no longer counts as touching (the bug's headline case), but the gap immediately AFTER the closing delimiter still does — the legitimate "just finished typing the closing marker" position. Exposed and fixed 4 existing tests that had conflated "1 Right-keypress" with "1 byte of movement." Submodule `2a9167af`.

**All 13 actionable Cluster K dogfood findings are now closed.** Re-enabling `CORBOMITE_PORT_BUILD_TESTS` (had silently drifted OFF in this build-dev cache, excluding `tests/app`/`tests/editor`/`tests/e2e` from every suite run this session until caught) surfaced one unrelated pre-existing failure, `tst_e2e_gui::testSaveShortcut` — logged as a new P2 punch-list entry, not fixed here (needs its own triage; deterministic, not caused by this session's changes, plausibly a Qt-6.11 regression of the 2026-05-26 watermark fix for the same test).

Also added a GPLv3 `LICENSE.txt` and expanded `README.md` (feature list, alpha-status/backup warning, Markoff/collabtext/Graffodil relationship) — commit `ad3491ba`.

`feature/find-replace` (which had accumulated Find/Replace, hover-preview re-light, and this whole Cluster K arc since 2026-06-12) merged into `master` at the user's request. This merge also reconciled with `master`'s own independent post-2026-06-14 work (the entries directly below — Find/Replace's own dogfood fixes, plus a subsequent `findSpans`-preserve fix and an EditorSuggest test-mock guard that had landed straight on `master` in a separate line of work) — submodule pin resolved to the newer/superset commit (`1fd3ef11`, a strict descendant of `master`'s prior `2a551cde` pin); no source-file conflicts, only these two docs needed manual reconciliation.

## 2026-06-14 — Find/Replace shipped (Phase 2 placebo removal) + two dogfood fixes

Built Replace for the editor on branch `feature/find-replace` (pushed to
Codeberg, **green and user-verified, merged to `master` 2026-08-17**). Spec
[`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-12-replace-find-ui-design.md`](superpowers/specs/2026-06-12-replace-find-ui-design.md),
plan [`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-06-12-replace-find-ui.md`](superpowers/plans/2026-06-12-replace-find-ui.md).

**Architecture (option C — minimal upstream primitive, consumer-owned UI).**
Two small Markoff additions: `MarkoffDocument::replaceMatches(QList<SearchHit>,
QString)` (maps block-local match offsets → global no-separator flat offsets via
`iterateBlocks()`/`blockText()`, applies descending-by-start so earlier edits
don't shift later ones, wraps the batch in one `UndoLog::Transaction` so a single
`undoD2()` reverses a Replace-All, then `flushPendingD2Changed()` for a
synchronous post-state) and the mutation-free `FindController::selectMatchAtOrAfter`
(re-anchors the selection past a replacement). `FindController` stays mutation-free
(invariant D3). Corbomite side: `FindBar` grew a replace row (`setReplaceMode`,
`replaceRequested`/`replaceAllRequested`); `NoteEditorWidget` owns the replace
orchestration + `showReplaceBar`; `MainWindow` added `onReplace` +
`KStandardAction::replace`; the hamburger **Find…/Replace…** placebos
(`MarkdownView.cpp`) are wired to host triggers (mirrors `setPdfExportTrigger`).
Literal-only replacement (regex backreferences explicitly out of scope, YAGNI).
Implemented subagent-driven (TDD per task, spec + code-quality review each).

**Subagent caught a spec bug:** `coalesceLastUndo()` acts only on the legacy
flat-buffer undo stack, not the D2 `UndoLog`; the implementer switched to a nested
`UndoLog::Transaction` (spec/plan corrected).

**Dogfood fix 1 — black find-highlights.** `Corbomite::Core::ThemeService` is
`#if 0`-disabled pending the theme port; its active *stub* returned an empty
`Markoff::Theme{}`, so every unset Slot fell back through `Theme::color()` to
`TextDefault` (a dark color). QML text survives via its own fallbacks, but the C++
`InlineHighlighter` reads slots directly → find-pass painted black (and inline
code/link/tag formats too). Fixed: the stub now returns a palette-based
`defaultLight()/defaultDark()`. Re-enabled `tst_theme_service` with a focused
regression guard. (`51294db2`.)

**Dogfood fix 2 — find-highlight stuck after a replace.** A real markoff-live bug:
`LiveBlockModel::applyOps` wholesale-assigned a fresh parse record (no `findSpans`)
on a text-changing edit, wiping the adapter-owned find spans **without** listing
`FindSpansRole` in the `dataChanged` roles — so the delegate kept painting stale
spans (old offset/length behind the new text), and the adapter's follow-up
`setFindSpans({})` no-op'd against the already-wiped value (uncleared by
needle-delete or Esc). Fixed upstream by preserving `findSpans` across the row
update (the same pattern already used for `inlineSpans`); falsifiable regression
test `apply_ops_preserves_adapter_owned_find_spans_on_text_change`. Markoff
`master` `c3b5070f`; Corbomite re-pinned (`49f8ac7a`).

**Known follow-ups:** `Markoff::Theme::defaultDark()` omits the search-highlight
slots (Markoff queue #14, dark half) — dark mode highlights fall back to the light
text color, not black. Part B of the plan is unstarted: Insert Table/Callout
dialogs apply-at-caret, and multi-snippet search results (`SQLiteIndex.cpp:461`).
Baseline: Corbomite 271/271 offscreen; Markoff fast suite green except the 3 known
queue-#10 reds.

## 2026-06-11 — Phase 2 hover preview re-light

Road-to-dogfood **Phase 2** "hover preview" item shipped on `master` across three commits (`2ac999cb`, `fff4bd4a`, `6999df1a`; test-double doc note `920b5547`). Executed via subagent-driven-development against the approved spec [`specs/2026-06-11-hover-preview-relight-design.md`](superpowers/specs/2026-06-11-hover-preview-relight-design.md) + plan [`plans/2026-06-11-hover-preview-relight.md`](superpowers/plans/2026-06-11-hover-preview-relight.md). Each task passed a spec-compliance review then a Qt code-quality review; a final holistic integration review confirmed the end-to-end seam.

`HoverPopover` had a complete, tested state machine (300 ms delay → Pending → Visible, 500 ms grace, Ctrl-pin, Esc/outside dismiss) but was **dark on both ends**: its content path was built on the retired `Markoff::Reading::ReadingView`/`EmbedRenderer` (so `m_view == nullptr`, `renderTarget` a no-op, the two render/pinning tests `if(FALSE)`-gated), and nothing ever called `scheduleShow()`. The key discovery was that the seams already existed — no upstream Markoff work, no submodule re-pin.

**Content path (Task 1, `2ac999cb`).** Swapped the retired API for `setRenderEngine(MarkdownRenderEngine*)` + `setResources(Core::VaultResourceProvider*)` + `previewPlainText()`, with a read-only frameless `QTextBrowser m_display` (QObject-parented). `renderTarget` now `splitTarget`s the target, `resolveEmbed`s the path to markdown bytes, renders via the engine and copies `RenderedDocument::toQTextDocument()->toHtml()` into the browser (ownership-safe — no `RenderedDocument` lifetime member). Unresolved/missing → typed `*(unresolved: …)*` placeholder; null engine → raw-markdown fallback; never crashes. Subpath (`#heading`/`#^block`) slicing deferred (whole note rendered). Re-enabled `tst_hover_popover_render` (rewritten, 3 slots) + `tst_hover_popover_pinning` (state machine untouched). The two `MainWindow.cpp` `setVault→setResources` call-site swaps were folded in here to keep the build green.

**Trigger (Task 2, `fff4bd4a`).** Two connects in `NoteEditorWidget` from the shared `Markoff::LinkService` — `linkHovered` → `scheduleShow(target, anchor)` (target = `act.page` else `act.rawText`; `LinkKind::External` and empty filtered out), `linkHoverLeft` → `linkHoverEnded()`. Because both the Live binding and the Styled reading leaf pump the same `m_linkService`, this single pair lights up **Live and Reading** at once; Source (Qutepart) deliberately does not participate (matches Obsidian). New end-to-end `tst_note_editor_widget_hover` (2 slots) drives the real service via `notifyHover`/`notifyHoverLeft` — genuine TDD red (state Hidden) → green.

**Host wiring (Task 3, `6999df1a`).** `MainWindow` sets the engine once at construction (reuses the stateless app-lifetime `m_cardRenderEngine`, also shared with canvas cards) and the per-vault `VaultScopedResources` on open / `nullptr` on close; dead `EmbedRenderer` comment blocks purged from ctor + dtor. Lifetime is sound: `m_cardRenderEngine` (member `unique_ptr`) is declared after `m_hoverPopover` so it outlives the QObject child, and the popover only dereferences the engine from `renderTarget` during live hover — never during teardown — so (unlike the canvas views) it needs no explicit clear.

**Runtime-resolution check.** The integration review specifically traced the target-string handoff: the bare name passed to `scheduleShow` (`"Target"`) resolves correctly because production `VaultScopedResources::resolveEmbed` normalizes `"Target" → "Target.md"`; the unit-test `InMemoryResources` doubles intentionally match bare keys and now carry a comment warning not to copy that into a real provider (`920b5547`).

Full offscreen suite **270/270** (excl. `benchmark`).

**Eyeball-confirmed 2026-06-12 (PARITY-MATRIX → ✅).** A live dogfood pass surfaced an apparent "hover does nothing in Live" report, root-caused via systematic debugging to a leaf asymmetry rather than a wiring bug: the shared `LinkService` is correctly set on both leaves (`NoteEditorWidget.cpp:72,272`) and the downstream pipeline is identical, but the two leaves differ in *when* they emit. Styled (Reading) `LinkInteraction::handleMove` emits `notifyHover` on plain mouse-move; Live `LiveView.qml` `onPositionChanged` only calls `hoverLinkAt`→`notifyHover` when **Ctrl is held** (`LiveView.qml:394`; Ctrl+click activates, Ctrl+hover previews — the order is hold-Ctrl-*then*-move). Net: **Reading = plain hover; Live = Ctrl-then-hover**, which matches Obsidian's own Page Preview default (hover in reading mode, Ctrl/Cmd in edit mode). **User decision: keep the Ctrl-gate** (Obsidian-faithful; avoids preview flicker while writing) — no code change. The spec's "always-on hover, no modifier gate" claim was a wrong premise for Live (true only for Reading) and is corrected in-place with a 2026-06-12 banner. Note for any future "always-on Live hover": it would require an upstream `markoff-live` `LiveView.qml` change (un-gate `onPositionChanged`) + a Corbomite re-pin, and the Live hover path is currently validated only by a slot-level test that calls `hoverLinkAt` directly, never through real QML mouse events (Markoff INVARIANT #5).

**Tracked follow-ups (out of scope):** subpath (`#heading`/`#^block`) slicing in the preview; optional setting to toggle Live hover gate (hover vs Ctrl-hover); nested-embed expansion in previews; hover over Source mode; child-popover chains from links inside a pinned preview; a `LinkKind::External`-ignored test slot + tighter render-timeout in the trigger test.
